| reason | bodies | example |
|---|---:|---|

| type with no Rust representation | 4 | `FxReader.FromStream<DynamicSpriteFont>(Stream)` FxReader.cs:117 |
| native property without a setter template | 3 | `Clipboard.GetClipboard()` Clipboard.cs:16 |
| constructor of the native base class has no template | 2 | `InternalContentManager.InternalContentManager(IServiceProvider)` XnbReader.cs:15 |
| ToString() of a non-primitive in a concatenation | 1 | `LoggingILGenerator.Emit(OpCode)` LoggingILGenerator.cs:30 |
| field of a type with no Rust representation | 1 | `DummyPipelineContext instance field initialisers` FxReader.cs:16 |
| member of System.Array on a value whose element type is not known statically | 1 | `ArrayComponent.Restore(Array, DeepCloneContext)` StateSnapshotDefinition.cs:133 |

## Host values that static initialisers and field initialisers need

A value-returning host member that is not implemented panics (`host::unimplemented_value`). When the caller is a static initialiser or a field initialiser the whole class is unusable until the member is mapped natively or given a value in the config (`stubs`).

| host member | initialisers that call it |
|---|---|
| `Steamworks.CSteamID.Nil (field)` | Lobby (fields) |
| `Steamworks.HAuthTicket.Invalid (field)` | NetClientSocialModule (fields) |
| `System.Delegate.CreateDelegate(Type, MethodInfo)` | DeepCloning (static) |
| `System.Diagnostics.Process.ProcessName.get` | WindowsPerformanceDiagnostics (static) |
| `System.Guid.Empty (field)` | EffectHandle (fields) |
| `System.Reflection.PropertyInfo.CanWrite.get` | SnapshotGenVars (static) |
| `System.Type.GetElementType()` | DeepClassCloning (static) |
| `System.Type.GetMethod(string, BindingFlags)` | DeepCloneCodegen (static), DeepCloning (static) |
| `System.Type.GetProperties(BindingFlags)` | SnapshotGenVars (static) |
| `System.Type.IsArray.get` | DeepClassCloning (static) |

XNA Vector2 operators the JIT inlined, evaluated as its code does (jit_bodies.tsv, jit_vec_temps.tsv): 3637 operator sites, 1276 of them with the results on the x87 stack; call sites of game methods a caller inlined with other operator answers, calling a `__vcN` twin (jit_vec_ctx.tsv): 56. Bodies whose float locals follow the real JIT's allocation (config jitFloatAlloc, jit_float_alloc.tsv; bodies counted per emitted form): 1355 (locals in memory 3694, on the x87 stack 1941, evaluation-stack values 148); left to the rule because the names do not match the decompiled method: 20.
